Acceptance criteria:
- [ ] Each boar has `health {hp: 4, max: 4}` (number in `beastConfig.js`). Sword light hit = 1, hard hit = 3 (existing `SWORD_CFG`), applied from `combat:hit {source: 'player', target: <boarId>, damage}`; any later source (fireball, SPELL-01a) uses the same path. A boar takes damage at most once per 12 steps (0.2 s).
- [ ] Hurt: every damaging hit flashes the boar white for 0.1 s (6 steps) and flinches it 0.25 s (no movement); a hit during `windup` cancels the charge (-> `recover`); **a light hit during `charge` or stagger only flashes (no cancel, D-034)**; **any damaging hit makes the boar chase** (aggro, even from behind). Hard-hit knockback stays the US-078d behaviour.
- [ ] Death at hp <= 0: brain stops at once, its body stops blocking the player within 0.1 s, it tips onto its side over 0.4 s and **lies as a corpse until looted (E, US-091a2) or 60 s pass**, then sinks 0.3 m with a `dust` burst (n 14) over 0.5 s and is hidden (mechanism per architecture.md 37.16: `voxel.hidden`, not removed). Emits `beast:died {id, x, y, z, cause: 'sword' | 'fire'}` exactly once.
- [ ] A dead or dying boar is not targetable: Z-lock (US-128) breaks, the sword and the fireball ignore it; the US-128 bar shows the real HP while it lives.
- [ ] Killed boars stay dead in the session; all boars come back alive at home with full HP after the player's death respawn (`beasts.resetAll`) and after a reload.
- [ ] Tests: hp math + damage cooldown, death timeline step counts, `beast:died` once, untargetable after death, resetAll restores, 600-step replay hash stable with a kill in it, no per-frame allocation.
- [ ] Owner look: 4 light hits on a boar -> 4 flashes, it falls over, dust, gone.
Design needed: yes - boar `hurt` flash + `die` tip-over (clips or a transform recipe, as 37.13 decides) + dust numbers (PC-A designer).
Notes / dependencies: **Needs US-079b0 (engine `voxel.hidden`) first; note is architecture.md 37.16 (not 37.13); `despawnCorpse(id)` API for the looter.** Original: (remove vs hide, respawn/resetAll, refresh of the sword + targeting target lists that are snapshotted at creation, hurt flash on a voxel entity). Slice of US-079 (hurt/die/respawn ACs); the US-079 drop AC moves to US-091a; light safe zone stays in US-079. Sprint 6 #1.

### US-079c Boar fight reads (folds BUG-BOAR-OVERLAP)  [Priority: P0] [Status: todo]  [PC-B game]
As a player, I want to see each boar coming and never see two boars melt into one, so that the fight is fair and readable.
Acceptance criteria:
- [ ] BUG-BOAR-OVERLAP fixed as its row says (hard pairwise de-overlap after `steer.step`, no wall tunnelling, deterministic, allocation-free; tests: two boars charging one point never closer than `rA + rB - 1e-3`, two spawned overlapping separate within 30 steps). Close BUG-BOAR-OVERLAP with this story.
- [ ] Notice: a `!` glyph (designer `ui.alert`, default yellow `#ffd23a`) 0.4 m above the head for the 0.6 s notice pause; readable at 12 m at 240x90.
- [ ] Windup (0.5 s): the boar backs 0.15 m, lowers its head (model pitch ~10 deg) and kicks a small `dust` burst (n 4) at the forefeet every 0.15 s; readable at 10 m.
- [ ] Only one boar is in `windup`/`charge` at a time; the other keeps 4-6 m away (circles or waits) until the charge ends.
- [ ] A charge hit costs the player 5 HP (current `beastDamageScale`), so 6 hits from full (30 HP) kill; the existing 1 s invulnerability stays.
- [ ] Tests for each AC above; 600-step replay hash stable.
- [ ] Owner walk-test: two boars, never merged, each charge is announced and can be sidestepped.
Design needed: small - `ui.alert` colour, scrape burst numbers (defaults above are fine).
Notes / dependencies: after US-079b (same file). If the de-overlap must live in `engine/nav/steer.js` -> `arch-review`. Sprint 6 #2.

### US-091a Loot drops + inventory data (UMBRELLA - split 2026-10-05 into US-091a1 + US-091a2; the ACs below are superseded where they differ)  [Priority: P0] [Status: todo]  [PC-B game]
As a player, I want a dead boar to leave loot that I can pick up into a pack, so that fighting is rewarding.
Acceptance criteria:
- [ ] Item defs come from `design/items.js` (designer): `sword`, `spell.fireball`, `torch` (def only), `boar.meat`, `boar.hide`, `boar.tusk`, each with id, name, kind (`weapon` | `spell` | `tool` | `food` | `material`), stack max (meat 10, hide 20, tusk 20, others 1), icon.
- [ ] `player.components.inventory {slots: [{id, n}] x 24, left, right}` (serialized). Taking the sword (`sword.take`) adds `sword` and sets `left = 'sword'` if empty; a new game starts with `spell.fireball` in the pack and `right = 'spell.fireball'` (owner question 2 may change where it comes from).
- [ ] On `beast:died`: drop table (seeded, deterministic) meat x1 100 %, hide 60 %, tusk 25 %, plus the existing US-080b HP/MP drop at 50 % (now with a caller). Drops pop out of the body (0.4 m hop, 0.3-0.6 m away), land on the ground, bob like pickups, live 60 s (blink the last 3 s); they share the pickups 16-slot cap.
- [ ] Walking within 0.8 m collects an item drop into the pack (stacks first, then the first empty slot); a toast top-centre "+1 Boar Meat" for 1.5 s (max 3 lines, newest at the bottom). Pack full: the drop stays and the toast says "Pack full".
- [ ] The pack and hands survive a save/load round trip and the player's death respawn (items are kept).
- [ ] Tests: drop table rolls with a fixed seed, stacking/full pack, collect radius, toast queue, serialize round trip, death keeps items; no per-frame allocation.
- [ ] Owner look: kill a boar, see meat (and sometimes hide/tusk) on the ground, walk over it, toast shows.
Design needed: yes - `design/items.js` (6 defs, placeholder names, ASCII icons), loot sprites `lootMeat` / `lootHide` / `lootTusk`, toast style (PC-A designer). Writer names/texts optional (placeholders OK).
Notes / dependencies: after US-079b (`beast:died`). Replaces the US-079 "50 % heart" drop AC. Unblocks the BUG-PICKUP-001 latent path (check it still passes). Sprint 6 #3.

### HANDS-01 Two hand slots, LMB/RMB, any item in either hand (UMBRELLA - split 2026-10-05 into HANDS-01a/b/c; 37.8a)  [Priority: P0] [Status: todo]  [PC-B cross-track]
As a player, I want my left mouse button to use what is in my left hand and the right button what is in my right hand, so that I can fight with a sword and a spell at the same time, in the hands I choose.
Acceptance criteria:
- [ ] `inventory.left` / `inventory.right` hold an item id or null (US-091a component; if US-091a is not merged yet, HANDS-01 creates the two fields and US-091a extends them).
- [ ] LMB drives the left-hand item, RMB the right-hand item; tap vs hold is owned by each item (sword 0.4 s / 24 steps, fireball 0.6 s / 36 steps; the router passes only raw down); the browser context menu never opens on the game canvas.
- [ ] The sword works in either hand: right hand = exact mirror of the left (pose, swing direction, trail, hit order: a right-hand light swing hits a left-forward target before a right-forward one). Generalises the BUG-VM-001 `sweepRtoL` flag to a `hand` parameter; no hard-coded side anywhere.
- [ ] Both hands draw at the same time through the TORCH-01a handles, each with its own bob; an empty hand draws nothing and its button does nothing. The spell hand shows the designer `spellHand` idle (cast/charge clips come with SPELL-01b).
- [ ] One item cannot be in both hands; putting it in the other hand empties the first. Dev key with `?debug=1`: `H` swaps the hands (until US-091b).
- [ ] gpucompare pose `handsSwapped` (sword right + spell hand left, mesh pitched): `vmOk` on both twins; the existing view-model rows do not regress (D-039 for new precision-only fails).
- [ ] Tests: input mapping, mirrored hit order, swap/move rules, view-model per-hand handles.
- [ ] Owner walk-test: sword left + ember right, then `H`: sword right swinging left-to-right, ember left.
Design needed: yes - `viewModels.spellHand` (open hand + ember glow, idle loop; authored for the left hand, mirrorable) (PC-A designer).
Notes / dependencies: **NEEDS PC-A: architecture.md 37.8 amendment "hands"** (per-hand handle + mirror at bind, sword sim `hand` param, RMB/context menu, input gate). After TORCH-01a ARCH OK and BUG-VM-001 left-hand published, and US-078d knockback merged (sword.js). Engine part -> `arch-review`. Sprint 6 #4. TORCH-01b becomes "torch as an item in either hand" on top of this.

#### HANDS-01 split (PO 2026-10-05; tech architecture.md 37.8a)
- **HANDS-01a** (first review, core render): `setHand(h, hand)` / `handOf(h)`, authored hand from `def.hand` or the rest-pose sign; mirrored list has det < 0 for every part and equals S * unmirrored within 1e-12 at 3 clip times incl. bob; `mountEye` x negated; mirror twice = identity; `item.mirror` reset in DrawList; frontFace flip (GPU) / cull sign (JS); JS mirrored sword with cull on is bit-identical to cull off; 2 handles, 1 mirrored -> 2 items, distinct objectIds; 0 alloc over 1000 frames; old view-model tests unchanged.
- **HANDS-01b**: `Mouse2` + `blockContextMenu(canvas)` (+ `input.test.js`); `sim/hands.js` router (L/R mapping, empty hand does nothing, gate close cancels a held charge with no cast/swing, re-arm only after button-up, one item never in both hands, swap); sword `setHand` with `ARC_BOUNDS_L/_R` (right hand hits a left-forward target earlier; mid-swing swap keeps the latched table; `sweepRtoL` removed); `startConfig.js` demo start (`?demo=0` = empty pack, no fireball); `H` swaps hands with `?debug=1`; 600-step replay hash stable; 0 alloc over 10k steps.
- **HANDS-01c**: spell-hand idle view (hidden when no spell in a hand, shown in the bound hand, own bob); gpucompare pose `handsSwapped` vmOk on both twins or a D-039 baseline, old rows do not regress; owner walk-test: sword left + ember right, then `H`.

### SPELL-01a Fireball sim  [Priority: P0] [Status: todo]  [PC-B game]
As a player, I want to throw a fireball from my spell hand, so that I can hit a boar from a distance with my mana.
Acceptance criteria:
- [ ] Tap the button of the hand holding `spell.fireball`: costs 5 MP (`vitals.spendMana`) **spent on release, once**; not enough MP -> the existing mana-short flash, nothing cast. Cooldown 0.5 s. **A charged release with only 5-9 MP casts a normal (tap) ball for 5 MP. A cast at the cap of 4 balls alive is refused without spending mana.**
- [ ] Hold >= 0.6 s then release = charged fireball: 10 MP, radius 3.0 m, damage 5, speed 12 m/s. Release before 0.6 s = normal tap cast.
- [ ] The fireball starts at the hand mount and flies along the crosshair aim at 16 m/s, no gravity, max 24 m (then it bursts in the air). Max 4 alive.
- [ ] It bursts on the first boar it touches (capsule radius + 0.2 m) or wall/terrain/prop collider, tested swept per step (no tunnelling).
- [ ] Burst: US-136 `explosionHits`, radius 2.0 m, damage 3 at the centre with linear falloff (direct hit min 1), walls block (LOS); each hit boar gets `combat:hit {source: 'player', target, damage, cause: 'fire'}` (hurt/death per US-079b) and a knockback impulse 6 m/s at the centre. The player gets knockback only (US-136 `applyImpulse`: ~6 m/s horizontal + 3 m/s up at the centre, scaled by the falloff) and **no damage** from the own fireball (owner answer 3).
- [ ] Origin = eye + hand offset from `viewModels.spellHand.castOffset` (D-042; a data test checks it against the designer's `ember` mount within 0.05 m), the ball steers to the crosshair point; a point-blank sweep eye -> origin bursts at once if a wall/boar is in the way.
- [ ] View hooks for SPELL-01b: one persistent trail emitter per ball (not one per step); fireball lights are bound at 5 m (cap in 37.14).
- [ ] Deterministic: 600-step replay hash stable with casts in it; no per-frame allocation.
- [ ] Tests: mana cost/refusal, tap vs charge, swept wall hit at 16 m/s, boar hit, falloff numbers, LOS block, no self-damage, cap 4.
Design needed: no (view = SPELL-01b).
Notes / dependencies: **NEEDS PC-A: architecture.md 37.14** (projectile sim game-side vs engine, swept `World.raySegment`, beast capsule test, `explosionHits` + `applyImpulse` for beasts and the player). After HANDS-01, US-079b, US-078d knockback. Sprint 6 #5.
